# Source Accessibility Screening Report

**Status: provisional parser-backed source-screening baseline; conservative source scope finalized.** All 3332 included sources received scores, but contextual rubric judgments remain unresolved. Both handwritten API landing pages are included because no actual publication exclusion is established. The external builder is unavailable, so inclusion is a documented source-audit assumption, not verification of live publication. This report is not a comprehensive expert evaluation and makes **no WCAG conformance claim**.

## Snapshot and Publication Scope

- Scan date: **2026-11-02 UTC**. **Source corpus provenance:** `3078ed57bf9e5e8d320019d2cbe2a9078f61ff39` (“Initial plan”), with parent `d0dfb23619ba0c26826808678c640b34780471c2` (PR #3239). The source corpus is unchanged from that baseline. Later audit-artifact commits are not source-checkout provenance. The audit writer did not commit or push.

- Recursively inventory `.md` and `.markdown` under the repository root, pruning `.git`, `.github`, `node_modules`, `_site`, `bin`, and `obj` **before reading**. No `.github/agents` access. Counts below cover the complete allowed traversal domain; inaccessible/pruned directory contents are intentionally not enumerated or assigned invented counts.

- `docs-builder.yml:34–36` contains the only configured Markdown exclusions: `**/README.md` and `**/copyright.md`, including root matches. Its snippet location is `./snippets` (line 16), and tabbed code is enabled (line 15). API `pdfHidden: true` affects PDF visibility only; `hideChildren`, navigation visibility and PDF visibility are not article-publication exclusions.

- Parse YAML frontmatter with installed **PyYAML 6.0.1**. Recognize `publish: false` and `published: false`, case-insensitively, including quoted values. **Missing publication flags or missing frontmatter default to inclusion**, unless another evidenced exclusion applies. Two files require trimming trailing whitespace for metadata parsing: `controls/radfiledialogs/features/error-handling.md` and `controls/raddiagram/extensions/settingspane/settingspane_overview.md`. Strict parse failures: **2**; successful whitespace-normalized recoveries: **2**; unresolved publication flags after recovery: **0**. Source bytes were not modified; normalization is recorded for human review, separately from article-scoring failures.

- Exclusion precedence: audit output → site excluded-files rule → unpublished frontmatter → confirmed include-only fragment. Each inventory path has exactly one inclusion/exclusion outcome. `_contentTemplates/ai-coding-assistant.md` and `_contentTemplates/glyphs-table.md` are section-delimited include fragments, confirmed by actual `@[template]` references, not standalone articles. Their contents were not included in article scores.

- **Handwritten API landing pages are included:** `_assetsApi/api/index.md` and `_assetsApiSL/api/index.md` contain CSS followed by a source H1 and prose, with no frontmatter. They are not generated API reference nodes. The scope researcher confirmed no actual file-publication exclusion is established for either path. `docfx/metadata-config.json` generates DLL-derived metadata, but that does not prove a handwritten landing page unpublished; API UID filtering is not a file-publication exclusion. `.gitignore` build/asset entries and underscore-prefixed directory names also do not establish exclusion. Under the missing-frontmatter/default-include rule, both pages are scored. **External builder source is unavailable**, so live publication behavior cannot be independently verified; that limitation remains in source-scope review entries. No generated API metadata was evaluated.

- Explicitly exclude this report and also mark it `publish: false`. `scores.jsonl` is not a Markdown article. Do not load include contents, external destinations or images. There are no additional false-published files after earlier exclusion reasons take precedence.

## Parser-backed Methodology

The `accessibility-doc-optimization` skill supplies eight rubric dimensions. Issue-specific weights, absent-type scores, rounding, severity constraints, output format and source-only limitations override its defaults. Installed **markdown-it-py 3.0.0** (CommonMark with its table rule), PyYAML and standard-library `HTMLParser` are used; no package installation or helper script files were needed. Regex is limited to publication directives, bounded textual screening heuristics and source cross-checks—not used as a substitute for the Markdown syntax parser or expert rubric judgment.

1. Retain source-byte SHA-256; evidence is **1-based physical LF source lines**, including frontmatter. Normalize CRLF for parsing and treat lone CR as whitespace, not a new physical source line. Nine articles contain 37 lone CR characters and receive review entries.

2. Blank frontmatter and known snippet/include/template/section directives while preserving line count. Detect and mask the ten local `{{region …}}` code regions as local code. Do not resolve `<snippet … />`, `[snippet]`, `@[template]`, include directives, or `#section`/`#end` markers; their source locations are queued for review, not prose/headings/code-language judgments. No include contents are loaded.

3. Blank only lines whose entire content is template directives; retain inline conditional image/link/prose/table content without selecting branches. Replace same-line Liquid expressions with reversible opaque tokens before Markdown parsing. This makes destinations such as `({%slug article-name%})` recognizable links despite whitespace. Restore source labels/destinations afterwards; do not evaluate Liquid or include expansion. Conditional source branches receive applicability-review entries.

4. Use Markdown parser tokens and source maps for ATX/Setext headings, table rows/cells, fenced/indented code, lists, inline code, images, inline/reference links, HTML blocks and inline HTML. Matching fence character/minimum closing length, opener-only language metadata, backtick/tilde fences and tabbed list fences are delegated to the parser. Code/HTML comments are not interpreted as headings, images, links or readability evidence.

5. Feed recognized HTML fragments and escaped inline text into **one persistent HTMLParser canvas with physical line positions**, preserving open HTML tables/anchors across Markdown token boundaries. Ignore `code`, `kbd`, `pre`, `script` and `style` bodies for prose. Detect actual `th` elements, not bold `td` labels. Markdown-like source links inside HTML data are screened separately and queued for Markdown-in-HTML applicability review; no rendered interpretation is assumed.

6. Check the **complete parser-recognized heading outline**, including every numbered/language-labelled figure, image, table or example caption and every heading adjacent to an example. Caption classification never removes a heading node or hierarchy transition. Compare every adjacent actual heading level: H2→H3 is sequential; an upward jump of more than one level is a skip. Do not invent an initial H1. `caption_headings` is purpose-review metadata only; `heading_outline_nodes` equals the heading count used in scoring. Caption-as-visual-styling questions receive separate contextual review, not an exemption from structure checks. Empty parent sections with child headings are not penalized; short links, identifiers, code, images and includes still count as content, including an image on the same lone-CR physical heading line.

7. Readability uses parser-extracted paragraph/list text only. Exclude code, raw syntax, headings, table data, URLs, filenames, inline-code APIs, numeric tokens, camel/Pascal-case identifiers and single emphasized capitalized API-like identifiers. Markdown link labels replace destinations; platform callout marker words are stripped before word counts. HTML-block prose is extracted separately, preserving blank paragraph boundaries and excluding tables; closing HTML tags cannot absorb subsequent Markdown paragraphs into readability evidence. Paragraph-start lines locate sentence/paragraph evidence. No reading grade or comprehensive active-voice/jargon assessment is claimed.

8. Inline conditional content is retained without branch evaluation (56 previously suppressed lines in 43 articles were restored to local screening); only directive-only lines are blanked. Recognize platform callout source markers independently from visual rendering; keep plain/emphasized variants for consistency screening. A source-open fence extending through EOF is parsed as code and separately reviewed, not counted as an ingestion failure. Source/custom-extension ambiguity is tracked in `human_review_required`.

### Transparent Score Mapping and Weights

All dimension scores are **integers 1–5**. Absent local content type → **5**. Present type starts at **4**, reserving 5 because contextual rubric checks have not been completed. Every present type has a contextual review entry. Formatting type means emphasis/style/underlining or color/formatting instructions; prose type means a screened paragraph with at least three alphabetic words; unresolved snippet content does not count as locally evaluated code.

For each present dimension: zero distinct configured pattern/line pairs → 4; one or two → 3; three or more → 2. A verified High finding caps that dimension at 2. This is a transparent operationalization for a provisional baseline, **not validated psychometric or WCAG severity-to-score equivalence**. Additional score-1 cases:

- Heading structure: at least four verified High heading findings. Alternative text: generic/filename or unresolved missing/empty-alt line counts exceed half the image-instance count; otherwise any such concern caps at 3 for up to two affected lines or 2 for more. An uncertainty-driven low score is **not** a severity finding or proof of blocked essential content.
- Links: recurring affected-line count exceeds half recognized link instances. Tables: verified headerless-table finding count exceeds half recognized tables. Code: unlabelled fenced openers exceed half recognized fenced blocks.

Recurring rules: one actual source heading-level skip → Medium; two or more skipped transitions in the complete outline → High for each transition, including caption-labelled headings. Multiple source H1s → High unless simultaneous publication is unresolved because of conditional branches. Caption purpose is reviewed separately; it never suppresses outline nodes. An exact vague label “Details,” “More Info/Information,” “Section N” → Medium; empty leaf section → Low; numbered instruction heading label over 30 source words, excluding the heading delimiter, → Medium. Generic image/image-number/screenshot/diagram/picture/photo/logo/figure labels and normalized numbered/file-style basename matches → Medium. Missing/empty alt with unknown purpose → human review only. Exact vague link labels (`here`, `click here`, `more`, `read more`, `this article/page/link`, `link`, `see here`, `more info/information`) → Medium; bare-URL labels → Low. Label/target consistency questions → review only.

Table empty-cell evidence → Medium; five-plus columns without four screened explanatory words in the preceding four source lines → Low. Parser padding/truncation and ambiguous raw pipe counts → review. Sentences with 26–40 screened words → Low; 41+ → Medium; paragraphs with 120+ → Medium. Selected `utilize/utilise`, `leverage`, `aforementioned`, `heretofore` forms, `you [will] need to`, and limited passive-by constructions → Low prompts. Possible unexpanded GAC/MSI/MVVM/MEF/ORM terms → review only, because audience knowledge is unresolved.

Mixed designated and plain/emphasized callouts → Medium; nonstandard syntax alone → Low; empty/“See above/below” opener → Medium pending continuation review. Contextually verify callout risk, completeness and overuse. Actual emphasis-only warning notices → Medium; nonlink underlining → Low; color/formatting-only reliance → review unless established. A “Warning Notification” example caption is not a warning notice. One language-less fenced opener → Medium; multiple → High. Code without four screened descriptive prose/caption words in its section, inspecting up to twelve candidates within 160 source lines → Low; adjacent language variants share an introduction. Placeholder consistency/outcomes → review only.

`overall_weighted_exact = Σ(integer_dimension_score × integer_percent_weight) / 100` is an unrounded numeric JSON field. `overall_score` separately displays that result with **HALF-UP, one decimal**. JSON numeric formatting may omit a trailing zero. Dimension means use integer scores; the repository mean uses exact article weighted scores, never a mean of rounded displays.

| Dimension | Weight | Score sum / sources | Repository mean |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| heading_structure | 20% | 12347 / 3332 | 3.7 |
| alternative_text | 20% | 13381 / 3332 | 4.0 |
| link_text_clarity | 15% | 13124 / 3332 | 3.9 |
| plain_language | 15% | 10489 / 3332 | 3.1 |
| table_accessibility | 10% | 15084 / 3332 | 4.5 |
| formatting_independence | 10% | 13671 / 3332 | 4.1 |
| callout_structure | 5% | 15441 / 3332 | 4.6 |
| code_block_accessibility | 5% | 16021 / 3332 | 4.8 |

**Repository overall: 3.9 / 5.** Exact mean: `1313615 / 333200 = 262723/66640` (approximately `3.942421968787515006002400960`), before display rounding. This aggregate uses the finalized conservative source scope, including both handwritten API landing pages; score judgments remain provisional.

The High findings are **305 headerless data/key-value tables, 690 heading findings, and 14 language-less fenced openers**. No essential-content access blocker was established from source, so no Critical severity was assigned. Zero verified Critical findings does not prove absence of blockers. Human-review entries may cover many lines and overlap dimensions; they are **not severity findings and not scoring failures**. Two strict YAML parse errors were recovered through whitespace normalization, as separately accounted above.

**Complete-outline correction:** all **15,058 heading nodes** participate in navigation checks, including **1,631 caption-labelled headings**. The complete outline contains **1,104 skipped-level transitions**, of which **1,069 target caption-labelled headings**. There are **677 High repeated-skip transitions and 13 High multiple-H1 findings**; **427 single-skip transitions are Medium**. Earlier draft triage that removed captions from hierarchy comparisons is superseded; no caption heading is silently omitted from the current navigation score. Caption-purpose/visual-styling judgments remain human-review questions, independent of the structural skips.

Unresolved cases include missing/empty image labels, image equivalence, conditional branches, source includes, label/target consistency, audience-dependent acronym expansion, unnumbered captions, Markdown-in-HTML behavior, and external-builder publication behavior. The API landing pages remain included under the confirmed conservative scope rule. `styling-and-appearance/styling-apperance-editing-control-templates.md:135` starts an EOF-extending fence: source is scored as code, but whether trailing “See Also” content was intended as prose remains unresolved.

## Validation and Manual Source Samples

**Current complete-outline validation is being refreshed.** Earlier parser and scope passes checked source inventories, parsing, hashes and source boundaries; their former caption-exclusion arithmetic is superseded. The refreshed validation checks every actual heading node and transition against an independent parser pass, the 3,332-record corpus, source hashes and line evidence, all dimension ranges/mapping/absent-type rules, exact arithmetic/HALF-UP displays, aggregate counts and complete report rows. No component tests/framework are run.

After scope confirmation, an additional **224,773-assertion** validation passed with zero failures. It independently reclassified the full inventory using actual frontmatter and the exact `excluded-files` section, confirmed both no-frontmatter API landing pages are included, and rechecked scores, arithmetic, evidence, report rows and the output-only change boundary. The publication-exclusion section was parsed in isolation because strict PyYAML parsing of unrelated full-builder metadata encounters existing trailing tabs; no configuration was modified, and no external-builder behavior was inferred.

Coverage is **3,337 = 3,332 scored + 5 exclusively excluded**, including both conservatively included handwritten API landing pages. All **26,656 dimension scores** are integers in 1–5, and every absent-type score is 5. All **1,009 report finding rows** match JSONL exactly in the required order. The exact weighted-score numerator is **1,313,615 hundredths**, divided by 3,332 sources before display rounding. JSONL has **3,332 UTF-8 object lines** with a terminal newline; source hashes and physical line references were checked. Only the two requested audit artifacts differ from the starting source revision.

Manual review reconfirmed the priority raw-source lines at `breaking-changes/2024-backward-compatibility.md:20,26`, `controls/radbuttons/features/pathbutton.md:42`, `controls/radexpressioneditor/expressions/constants.md:39`, and `controls/raddatafilter/localization.md:39`. Caption-labelled headings are also checked structurally: `controls/radtilelist/features/selection.md:38` is a single H2→H4 skip (Medium), while `controls/radgauge/getting-started/creating-linear-gauge.md:45,59,71` and `controls/radtabbedwindow/key-properties.md:20,28,56,76,88,100,122,136` contain repeated H2→H4 skips (High). `controls/radchart/how-to/howto-display-labels-and-tooltips-for-annotations3.md:17,153` contains instruction headings with H1/H2→H5 skips. An H2→H3 caption remains a heading but is not a skipped level. Short “See Also” link lists still count as section content, not empty sections.

All five repeated-language-less-fence articles were manually sampled: `installation/quick-start-setup.md:22,28,34,44,56`; `installation/manual-setup-guides/installation-guide.md:27,33,39`; `installation/licensing/installing-license-key.md:52,58`; `installation/manual-setup-guides/nuget/nuget-installation.md:42,48`; `installation/manual-setup-guides/nuget/nuget-keys.md:99,105`. These are current opening fences, including tab indentation—not bare closing fences.

Table samples: `knowledge-base/kb-desktopalert-change-background-color.md:15–26` (key-value metadata); `controls/raddatafilter/localization.md:39` (malformed resource table); `controls/radpropertygrid/localization/localization.md:37` (`td` labels); `controls/radpdfviewer/customization-and-extensibility/customize-pdf-rendering.md:41–68` (a malformed table split across Markdown tokens, preserved by persistent HTML parsing); `knowledge-base/kb-carousel-flat-image-gallery.md:15–17` (recognized Markdown headers, no headerless-table finding).

Human-review samples: `knowledge-base/kb-installation-could-not-load-file-or-assembly-demos.md:26–30` (empty alt with nearby error text); `controls/radchartview/indicators/indicators-exponentialmovingaverageindicator.md:22–23` (HTML image, missing alt, purpose unresolved); `ai-assistant/overview.md:32–37` (template directives); `controls/radcombobox/features/additional-content.md:20–37` (local native code region); `controls/radchartview3d/axes.md:28,36` (list indentation/lone CR); `controls/radscheduleview/localization.md:43` (emphasized Note). The “Warning Notification” caption at `controls/radnotifyicon/features/balloon-notifications.md:53` is not treated as an emphasis-only warning.

In-memory parser-recognition checks cover C# openers, matching/longer backtick/tilde closing fences, a too-short non-closing tilde fence, tabbed list fences, inline-code/comment/indented-code exclusion, reference images/links, and HTML headings/tables. These are audit validation probes, not component tests. Parser tokens recognize these structures; contextual meaning remains separately unresolved.

A focused site-syntax probe also verified that `[setup instructions]({%slug foo%})` and `[API details]({% slug bar %}#section)` become recognized links only after parser-only placeholder normalization, and that restoring destinations preserves their original Liquid text. Both links stayed on physical source line 3; heading lines 1 and 9 stayed unchanged; template/snippet directives on lines 5 and 7 were recognized without expansion. Source newline count was unchanged.

Actual-source line fidelity was also checked after frontmatter blanking and Liquid normalization: `getting-started/first-steps-nuget.md` retains heading lines 23/43/51/68, fenced opener lines 31/37/62/74/80/104, and site-link lines 27/41. `ai-assistant/overview.md` retains heading lines 28/35 and unexpanded template directive lines 32/33/37. The glyph reference sheet retains heading lines 69/77, site-link lines 79/80 and its unexpanded directive at line 75. These parser maps were compared with the current source lines manually sampled above; no included fragment content was opened for this verification.

### Historical Issue Context: Current-source Checks Only

The caller supplied MCP confirmation that issue **#3254 is closed and PR #3255 was merged**. That is historical context, not evidence that every fix exists in this checkout or that current findings must be zero. The audited source snapshot is `3078ed5` (“Initial plan”), whose parent is `d0dfb23` (PR #3239); the subsequent observed commit affects audit artifacts only. Findings are based on current source bytes and hashes, not inferred merge ancestry or a historical report.

The specifically named examples were checked afresh:

- `getting-started/first-steps-nuget.md:31,37,62,74,80,104`: all six current fenced openers have language tags; closing fences are not counted as unlabelled examples.
- `installation/manual-setup-guides/visual-studio-extensions/extensions-overview.md:12,30–48`: one H1, descriptive non-heading figure captions and an H2 “Visual Studio Support” section; no current heading-skip finding.
- `controls/radchartview/indicators/indicators-bollingerbandsindicator.md:12,24–32`: a source H1, an anchor and bold figure caption, then H2; no current heading skip. Image meaning is still contextual, not a rendered-behavior evaluation.
- `controls/radchartview/getting-started/data-point.md:28–37,147–171`: the source explains “Not applicable,” has semantic column headers, and uses explicit values rather than empty cells in the sampled rows. No current headerless-table or empty-cell pattern is assigned to this table. Its closing `</table>` at line 174 is not prose-readability evidence.
- `common-information/troubleshooting/invisible-controls.md:12,24,49,64`: exactly one source H1 and subordinate H2 sections; no current multiple-H1 finding.

These observations do not attribute any change to PR #3255. Other current sources still contain verified issues—for example, localization tables at `controls/raddatafilter/localization.md:39` and `controls/radpropertygrid/localization/localization.md:37` have `td` labels without semantic `th` headers. The closed issue/comment therefore **does not imply zero current findings**. No historical media behavior or template contents were imported into this audit.

## Limitations and Status

This is a defensible, bounded baseline combining reliable syntax parsing with deterministic screening and targeted manual review—not thousands of completed expert rubric evaluations. Scores remain provisional because contextual checks are unresolved. Conservative source scope is finalized: missing flags/frontmatter default to inclusion, the two handwritten API landing pages have no evidenced publication exclusion, and the two include-only templates are not standalone articles. Parser source maps improve recognition, but CommonMark/table parsing is not proof of the site's custom Markdown/template/HTML behavior. External builder source is unavailable, so the audit cannot verify undocumented later publication filtering or live-site inclusion; that limitation does not justify omitting handwritten sources.

**Shared-template blind spot:** both `_contentTemplates/ai-coding-assistant.md` and `_contentTemplates/glyphs-table.md` are excluded as standalone fragments, and host includes are **not expanded** under the source-only rule. Consequently, tables/images supplied by those shared fragments are **not assessed in their hosts or as standalone content**. This affects, for example, `ai-assistant/overview.md:32–37` and `styling-and-appearance/glyphs/common-styles-appearance-glyphs-reference-sheet.md:75`. Only locally authored host content is scored; a host's description of included content is not verification of that content. An absent-local-type score of 5 therefore does not certify the assembled article's included tables/images. The corresponding host review entries explicitly identify this coverage gap. No inspection or expansion of included content is used to close it; a future, separately scoped shared-content audit would be needed.

**Media-behavior exclusion:** GIF/video playback, autoplay, looping, motion, flashing, and pause/stop behavior are outside this source-only audit and receive **no Critical/High/Medium/Low severity findings**, including when mentioned historically in issue #3254. Source alt text and link-label patterns remain in scope independently of media file extension; they do not establish playback behavior or its accessibility.

No rendered behavior, DOM/ARIA/accessibility tree, keyboard/screen-reader experience, include contents, external page content, image pixels/contrast, code correctness, working examples or WCAG conformance was evaluated. Image/file presence and link reachability were not tested. Direct GitHub CLI/API retrieval was unavailable; the caller's MCP-confirmed issue/PR status is acknowledged only as historical context above. No historical report, PR implementation content, media behavior or template contents were used to infer current results. The supplied issue requirements and exact checkout sources remain the audit contract.

Only root `accessibility-report.md` and `scores.jsonl` are written. No dependencies were installed, no helper scripts/other repository files were created, no documentation/config was changed, and the audit writer did not commit or push. Final source-integrity checks compare against `3078ed57bf9e5e8d320019d2cbe2a9078f61ff39`, not merely the concurrent artifact-only commit.
